Result: 三种导航导出实现完全一致的成功率（1.000）和平均路径比（0.898），但表示级linkability差异达0.532-0.970。用目标区域替代显式目标标签使目标类别宏F1从1.000降至0.077而成功率保持0.995；几何粗化使物体类别宏F1从0.704降至0.556但带来可测的碰撞效用代价。ProcTHOR复现保持了导航任务等价/隐私不等价的核心发现，但改变了归一化和拓扑导出的相对排序。

Conclusion: 结果表明，无论是字段移除还是更强的抽象化，都不存在通用的隐私排序。隐私与效用的权衡高度依赖具体任务，因此应对完整公共表示进行任务特定、多风险的系统性评估，而非依赖单一规则或字段级删减。

Abstract: Domestic robots rely on rich perception to operate in private homes, but privacy risk persists even when raw sensor data remain local. Structured representations exported to downstream planners, cloud services, logs, or learning pipelines can still reveal household information through semantics, geometry, spatial structure, and task targets. We introduce Task-Functional Perception Distillation (TFPD), a task-scoped representation-export framework that keeps rich perception local and profiles downstream exports according to task utility, direct exposure, and multiple residual inference risks. Using 120 AI2-THOR scenes with scene-disjoint train/validation/test splits, frozen attacker selection, and representation-aware held-out attacks, we evaluate navigation, collision checking, and object-goal execution. Three navigation exports achieve identical success (1.000) and mean path ratio (0.898), yet representation-level linkability ranges from 0.532 to 0.970. Replacing an explicit target label with a target region reduces target-category macro-F1 from 1.000 to 0.077 while preserving success at 0.995, while geometric coarsening reduces object-category macro-F1 from 0.704 to 0.556 at a measurable collision-utility cost. A ProcTHOR replication preserves the navigation task-equivalence/privacy-inequivalence finding while changing the relative ordering of normalized and topological exports. These results show that neither field removal nor stronger abstraction induces a universal privacy ordering and motivate task-specific, multi-risk evaluation of the complete public representation.

</details>


### [333] [GPU-Accelerated Astrodynamics World Models for Spacecraft Rendezvous and Proximity Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03067)
*Duncan Eddy,Isaac R. Ward,Grace Ra Kim,Mykel J. Kochenderfer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次将世界模型（World Model）范式应用于航天器交会与临近操作领域。作者开源了基于JAX的ISS对接仿真环境（支持GPU并行），并提出了"Out-of-this-World-Model"——一种基于Transformer的世界模型，通过一步流匹配（one-step flow matching）在潜在空间中预测状态演化，在自主对接任务中较DreamerV3和强化学习基线取得了更高的对接成功率（53% vs 29%）、更好的分布外泛化能力（40% vs 17%）以及98%的异常物体分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型在机器人和游戏环境中已展现出强大能力，但尚未应用于太空领域。航天器交会与临近操作（RPO）涉及复杂的轨道/姿态动力学和相机图像观测，传统强化学习方法样本效率低且泛化能力有限。作者旨在将世界模型范式引入太空领域，以支持多步规划、轨迹预测及不确定性估计，从而提升自主对接任务的性能。

Method: （1）构建了基于JAX的ISS对接仿真环境，支持GPU并行模拟轨道和姿态动力学，能够高效生成世界模型训练所需的大量状态-动作转移数据；（2）提出"Out-of-this-World-Model"，一种Transformer架构的世界模型，将相对运动学状态与体装相机图像编码为潜在状态，利用一步流匹配预测该潜在状态在校正推力和扭矩下的演化，输出未来观测的概率分布以捕捉随机动力学和逐时间步不确定性；（3）在保持排除区约束下的自主对接场景中评估该方法，并与DreamerV3式后验校正基线和强化学习基线进行对比。

Result: 该方法实现了53%对接成功率（对比RL基线29%），在域外停泊端上成功率翻倍以上（40% vs 17%），异常物体检测分类准确率达98%；同时参数量与超参数少于DreamerV3式基线。

Conclusion: 世界模型范式在太空自主对接中展现了优于强化学习基线的样本效率、任务性能和分布外泛化能力，且具备不确定性估计与异常检测能力。作者开源仿真环境和模型架构，期望推动该范式在太空领域进一步研究。

Abstract: World models are an emerging paradigm in representation learning in which an agent jointly learns state-action dynamics and observation models from offline trajectory data, enabling multi-step planning and trajectory prediction with uncertainty estimates. They have shown strong results in robotics and game environments, but, to the best of our knowledge, have not previously been applied to the space domain. This paper introduces a world model-based approach to cooperative and non-cooperative spacecraft rendezvous and proximity operations. First, we introduce an open-source, JAX-based International Space Station (ISS) docking environment supporting parallel GPU simulation of spacecraft orbit and attitude dynamics, generating the thousands of state-action transitions that world model training requires. Second, we introduce Out-of-this-World-Model, a transformer-based world model that encodes relative kinematic states and body-fixed camera imagery into a latent state and predicts its evolution under commanded thrusts and torques using one-step flow matching. It produces a distribution over future observations, capturing stochastic dynamics and per-timestep uncertainty, and outperforms DreamerV3-style posterior-correction baselines with fewer trainable parameters and hyperparameters. Third, we apply the approach to a capsule autonomously docking with the ISS under keep-out-zone constraints, demonstrating improved sample efficiency and task performance over reinforcement learning baselines (53% versus 29% docking success across ports), better out-of-distribution generalization (on held-out ports the world model more than doubles baseline success, 40% versus 17%), and detection of anomalous objects encountered during approach with 98% classification accuracy. We open-source the simulation environment and model architecture to enable further study of this paradigm.

</details>


### [334] [Sensing Which Modality Matters: Evidence-Gated Regularization for Robust VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03142)
*Yue Yang,Diego Romeres,Chiori Hori,Gedas Bertasius,Daniel Szafir,Siddarth Jain*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出证据门控正则化（EGR）方法，用于解决视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略中的"模态纠缠"问题——即有限且同质化的机器人演示数据导致模型过度依赖传感器间虚假相关性而非任务相关信号。EGR通过推理零开销的训练目标，利用逐帧逐传感器任务相关性信号来门控两个状态条件一致性目标：低证据传感器的不变性与高证据传感器的单一传感器充分性。论文在基于BEHAVIOR-1K的新基准、双Kinova臂+三RGB相机的双臂设置，以及配备视觉和GelSight触觉传感器的单臂MELFA ASSISTA设置上验证了该方法，模拟成功率提升31%-120%，实物实验成功率提升27%-183%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略通常基于有限且同质化的机器人演示数据进行训练，这容易鼓励模型学习传感器间的虚假相关性而非任务相关的真实信号，作者将此失败命名为"模态纠缠"。在真实世界的遮挡和干扰物下，模态纠缠导致模型对无信息传感器的损坏表现出不必要的敏感性，而当仅剩一个有信息的传感器完好时又会出现单一模态信息不足的问题。现有方法缺乏针对这一系统性故障的解决方案和评估工具。

Method: 提出证据门控正则化（EGR）框架，这是一种模态无关的训练目标。具体包括：(1) 为每帧每个传感器推导一个任务相关性（证据）信号；(2) 基于该证据信号门控两种状态条件一致性目标——低证据传感器上施加不变性约束，高证据传感器上施加单一传感器充分性约束；(3) EGR在推理时零额外开销。同时构建了基于BEHAVIOR-1K的基准，包含快速推理诊断套件和47个针对模态纠缠的rollout技能，并设计了来自实际机器人设置（双臂和单臂触觉）的评估。

Result: 模拟实验中，EGR将全模态成功率从12.5%提升至16.4%（+31%），无信息传感器损坏下从9.4%提升至16.5%（+75%），单传感器回退下从2.8%提升至6.1%（+120%）。实物实验中，物理干扰物情境下EGR将双臂设置成功率从30%提升至85%（+183%），触觉设置从55%提升至70%（+27%）。所有改进均在多种实施例（双臂视觉系统和单臂视觉-触觉系统）和多种干扰条件下得到验证。

Conclusion: EGR通过证据门控正则化有效缓解了VLA策略中的模态纠缠问题，在不引入推理开销的前提下显著提升了模型在传感器损坏、遮挡和干扰物等现实条件下的鲁棒性。该方法模态无关，可适用于不同实施例的多种传感器组合，为机器人VLA策略的可靠部署提供了有效手段。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies fuse multimodal sensory inputs, but training on limited and homogeneous robot demonstrations encourages spurious inter-sensor correlations rather than task-relevant signal, a failure we term modality entanglement. Under real-world occlusions and distractors, this manifests as nuisance sensitivity to corruption of uninformative sensors and single-modality insufficiency when only one informative sensor remains intact. We propose Evidence-Gated Regularization (EGR), a modality-agnostic training objective that introduces zero inference-time overhead. EGR derives a per-frame and per-sensor task-relevance signal to gate two state-conditional consistency objectives: invariance on low-evidence sensors, and single-sensor sufficiency on high-evidence ones. We introduce a benchmark based on BEHAVIOR-1K, comprising a fast inference-only diagnostic suite and 47 rollout-based skills targeting modality entanglement. We validate EGR on this benchmark and on two real-robot setups with fundamentally different embodiments: a bi-manual setup with two Kinova arms and three RGB cameras, and a single-arm MELFA ASSISTA setup combining vision and GelSight tactile sensors. EGR improves simulation success rates (SR) from 12.5% to 16.4% under full modalities (+31%), from 9.4% to 16.5% under uninformative-sensor corruption (+75%), and from 2.8% to 6.1% under single-sensor fallback (+120%). Under physical-object distractors, EGR boosts SR from 30% to 85% on the bi-manual setup (+183%) and from 55% to 70% on the tactile setup (+27%).

</details>


### [335] [Real-Time Shape Control of Multi-Segment Soft Robotic Arms Using Koopman Operators with Global and Local Observables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03175)
*Jiahe Wang,Eron Ristich,Sultan Haidar Ali,Eric weissman,Lei Zhang,Wanxin Jin,Yi Ren,Jiefeng Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Koopman算子模型预测控制框架，结合全局与局部可观测变量，实现多段软体机器人手臂的实时形状控制，并通过数值与物理实验验证其在最多10段、3/5段实物上的可扩展性、鲁棒性和动态性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多段软体机器人手臂的尖端控制不足以满足受限空间任务需求，而形状控制虽更为关键，却因连续体变形的高维性和非线性动力学而极具挑战。现有方法仅使用全局框架下的形状误差作为控制目标，无法充分处理段间耦合、重力载荷和惯性效应，且问题随段数增加而加剧。

Method: 提出Koopman-based模型预测控制（MPC）框架，将全局与局部可观测变量结合，利用Koopman算子对非线性动力学进行线性化嵌入，实现实时形状控制。通过数值与物理实验评估：数值实验测试最多10段独立驱动，物理实验验证3段与5段机械臂在最高0.6 m/s尖端速度下的实时形状控制。

Result: 数值实验展示了控制器在10段机器人上的可扩展性；物理实验验证了：(1) 3/5段机械臂在0.6 m/s尖端速度下的实时形状控制能力；(2) 无需重训练的鲁棒跟踪，支持400g末端载荷和7N侧向扰动的恢复；(3) 受限空间内的检测应用潜力。

Conclusion: 该Koopman-MPC框架实现了多段软体机械臂的动态、可扩展和精确的实时形状控制，有望支持受限空间中的实际部署任务。

Abstract: Multi-segment soft robotic arms can continuously reconfigure their body shapes for safe interaction, but tip control alone is insufficient for constrained-space tasks. Therefore, shape control is a more important task for multi-segment soft arms than tip control, but remains challenging due to the high dimensionality and nonlinear dynamics of continuum deformation. In existing work, shape control accuracy is defined by the error in the global frame (global shape error). For multi-segment soft arms, using only global shape error as the control objective is insufficient, as segment coupling, gravity-induced loading, and inertial effects become more significant. This difficulty increases with the number of segments. In this paper, we present a Koopman-based model predictive control framework that combines global and local observables, enabling real-time shape control on multi-segment soft robotic arms. The framework is evaluated through numerical and physical experiments. Numerical experiments demonstrate the scalability of the proposed controller by achieving shape control on robots with up to 10 independently actuated segments. The physical experiments demonstrate that the controller is capable of (1) real-time shape control of 3- and 5-segment robotic arms with tip speeds up to 0.6 m/s, (2) robust tracking without retraining, including distal payloads up to 400~g and recovery from a 7~N lateral disturbance, and (3) the potential for future inspection applications through a confined-space demonstration. These results demonstrate that the proposed framework enables dynamic, scalable, and accurate real-time shape control on multi-segment soft robotic arms.

</details>


### [336] [Following a Unique Path: A Fast Certifier Applied to Outlier-Robust Pose Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03222)
*Connor Holmes,Abhishek Goudar,Timothy D. Barfoot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CP-Cert（中心路径认证器），一种针对非凸问题中特定退化性松弛设计的可认证方法。该方法从候选解出发，在可行空间中寻找中央路径附近的区域以获得有效证书，并通过间接线性代数技术、稀疏性和并行性保持高效性。应用于矩阵加权位姿配准和点云数据关联，运行速度比现有最先进直接求解器快最多三个数量级，并集成到可认证的鲁棒位姿估计流程中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可认证方法通常采用"局部求解-认证"两阶段策略，利用SDP松弛和高效线性代数技术来保证非凸问题的全局最优性。然而，许多机器人学问题中，这种松弛存在特定的退化性（degeneracy），导致局部求解后无法顺利进行后续认证，只能退回成本高昂的松弛优化。作者希望解决这一瓶颈，使退化情形下也能快速、高效地进行全局最优性认证。

Method: CP-Cert的核心思路是从候选解出发，在可行空间中寻找靠近"中央路径"（central path）的区域——在该区域中可以获得有效的认证证书。具体实现上：利用间接线性代数技术、问题稀疏性和并行计算来提高效率；将该方法应用于两个问题的新颖SDP松弛：矩阵加权位姿配准和点云数据关联（后者提出的松弛本身具有独立研究价值）。

Result: 在模拟实验中，CP-Cert展现了快速和可扩展性，运行时间比最先进的直接求解器快最多三个数量级。同时探索了该新颖SDP松弛的性质。最后将这些贡献整合为可认证的、对离群点鲁棒的位置估计流程，并在真实数据上进行了验证。

Conclusion: CP-Cert为退化性SDP松弛下的可认证优化提供了一种高效的解决方案，显著降低了计算成本，并通过真实数据实验证明了其在鲁棒位置估计流水线中的实用价值。

Abstract: Certifiable methods have arisen as a means to guarantee global optimality of solutions to non-convex problems using convex semidefinite programming (SDP) relaxations. The most performant of these methods use a local solver to obtain the candidate solution, and then certify its optimality using efficient linear algebra techniques. However, for many problems of interest in robotics, this local-solve-then-certify approach is impeded by a form of degeneracy in the relaxation, leaving a costly optimization of the relaxation as the only recourse. In this paper, we introduce our Central-Path Certifier (CP-Cert), a certifiable method explicitly tailored to certify candidate optima to problems that exhibit this form of degeneracy. Using a candidate as a starting point, our approach seeks a nearby region of the feasible space -- known as the central path -- where a valid certificate can be readily obtained. The approach is kept efficient by exploiting indirect linear algebra techniques, problem sparsity, and parallelism. We apply CP-Cert to both matrix-weighted pose registration and pointcloud data association, whose novel SDP relaxation is of independent interest. On simulated examples, we explore the properties of this novel relaxation and show that CP-Cert is fast and scalable, achieving runtimes that are up to three orders of magnitude faster than state-of-the-art direct solvers. Finally, we combine these contributions into a certifiable, outlier-robust pose-estimation pipeline, which we apply to real-world data.

</details>


### [337] [Long-Horizon Consistent and Interaction-Aware World Models for Multi-Style End-to-End Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03225)
*Yuxuan Han,Kunyuan Wu,Liyunong Yang,Zilu Wang,Cansen Jiang,Yi Xiao,Liang Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: StyleDrive是一个基于世界模型的端到端自动驾驶学习框架，通过时间一致性正则化、显式状态解耦和多风格策略优化三大模块，解决了现有世界模型在长时程想象展开、主车-环境交互建模以及多样化驾驶风格适应方面的局限，在Bench2Drive基准上达到88.44驾驶分数和66.82%成功率（分别超过此前最佳世界模型方法17.08和16.58），并在真实AGV平台上验证了仿真到现实的迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于世界模型的端到端自动驾驶强化学习框架存在三个关键局限：1) 长时程想象展开中的时间不一致性导致误差累积；2) 对主车与环境之间交互状态的建模不充分；3) 难以适应多样化驾驶风格。这些局限限制了世界模型在复杂动态交通场景中的学习效率和泛化能力。

Method: StyleDrive采用统一的联合学习范式，包含三个核心模块：1) 利用门控交叉注意力整合历史潜在状态的时间一致性正则化，稳定长时程想象展开并缓解误差累积；2) 显式状态解耦模块，分离主车相关与主车无关的交互交通状态，提升复杂场景中决策的可解释性和效率；3) 采用Group Relative Policy Optimization实现多样化的驾驶行为，用轨迹级别的相对优势替代逐步骤奖励优化，降低奖励方差并支持多种驾驶风格而无须重新训练。

Result: 在Bench2Drive闭环驾驶基准上实现88.44驾驶分数（相较此前最优世界模型方法提升17.08）和66.82%成功率（提升16.58）。此外，在真实AGV平台上部署后展示了在动态驾驶场景中良好的仿真到现实迁移能力。

Conclusion: StyleDrive通过联合建模长时程一致性、解耦交互状态和采用群体相对策略优化的统一框架，显著提升了世界模型在自动驾驶中的学习效率和驾驶性能，同时验证了其在真实硬件平台上的可行性，为世界模型驱动的自动驾驶提供了新的方法论路径。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving has increasingly adopted world model-based reinforcement learning frameworks to improve learning efficiency through \textit{imagined rollouts}. However, existing world models suffer from three key limitations: temporal inconsistency in long-horizon imagined rollouts, inadequate modeling of ego-environment interactions, and limited adaptability to diverse driving styles. To address these challenges, we propose \textit{StyleDrive}, a world-model-based learning framework that jointly enforces long-horizon consistency, explicitly disentangles interactive traffic states, and supports multi-style policy optimization within a unified learning paradigm. First, we introduce a temporal consistency regularization that integrates historical latent states through gated cross-attention, stabilizing long-horizon imagined rollouts and mitigating error accumulation. Second, we design an explicit state disentanglement module that separates ego-relevant from ego-irrelevant interactive states, enabling more interpretable and efficient decision-making in complex traffic scenarios. Third, we enable multi-style driving behaviors through Group Relative Policy Optimization, which replaces per-step reward optimization with trajectory-wise relative advantages, reducing reward variance and supporting diverse driving styles without retraining. We evaluate StyleDrive on the Bench2Drive closed-loop driving benchmark, achieving a driving score of 88.44 (+17.08 over the previous best world model-based method) and a success rate of 66.82 (+16.58). Furthermore, we deploy StyleDrive on a real automated guided vehicle platform and demonstrate promising sim-to-real transfer capability in dynamic driving scenarios.

</details>


### [338] [Establishing a Dynamic Multimodal HRI Dataset for Engagement Analysis with a Humanoid Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03255)
*Buwan Kim,Wonse Jo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于分析人机交互（HRI）中用户参与度的多模态数据集构建实验设计，整合可穿戴生理信号、行为数据和自我报告测量，并定义了不同任务复杂度水平下的数据采集协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往的人机交互用户参与度研究主要依赖可观察的行为线索，缺乏整合生理信号的框架，因此需要提出一个更全面的多模态数据收集方案。

Method: 提出一个结构化的数据采集协议，用于构建包含可穿戴生理信号、行为数据以及自我报告测量的多模态数据集，并在实验中定义不同水平的任务复杂度来系统性地收集数据。

Result: 论文呈现的是实验设计本身，属于研究方案层面的输出；具体结果尚未在摘要中呈现，核心贡献在于提供了多模态数据集构建的协议框架。

Conclusion: 本研究通过整合生理、行为与自我报告三类数据源，在不同任务复杂度条件下建立多模态数据集，为后续更全面地分析HRI中的用户参与度提供实验设计基础。

Abstract: This paper presents an experimental design for constructing a multimodal dataset to analyze user engagement in human-robot interaction (HRI). Prior studies have mainly relied on observable behavioral cues, with limited frameworks integrating physiological signals. We therefore propose a structured data-collection protocol to build a multimodal dataset that includes wearable physiological signals, behavioral data, and self-report measures under different levels of task complexity defined in this experiment.

</details>


### [339] [R2S-Eval: Robot Evaluation with Real-to-Sim Calibration via Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03276)
*Yidi Wang,Feixiang Ruan,Ruoqu Chen,Jie Yin,Yang Yu,Mengdi Xu,Kaifeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出R2S-Eval评测管线，结合真实到仿真校准（real-to-sim）与视觉语言模型（VLM）偏好评估，用于评估机器人操作策略。该方法通过仿真生成rollout视频，利用VLM评估执行质量并产出成对偏好，进而聚合为策略排名，减少重复硬件实验负担并提供超越传统成功率指标的细粒度行为质量评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着通用VLA模型部署到物理机器人，传统真实世界评测方法劳动密集、结果不稳定且信息量有限：需要反复硬件试验、人工场景重置和持续监控，可能在不同评测轮次间产生不一致的策略排名，且过度依赖成功率这一二值指标，无法反映执行质量。而人类评估机器人时是基于观察完整行为并进行比较，而非仅看成败结果。

Method: R2S-Eval由两部分组成：（1）real-to-sim校准组件，将仿真器校准到真实世界评测环境，高效生成rollout视频，减少重复硬件试验；（2）VLM评估组件，对rollout视频的执行质量进行评估并生成成对偏好，再将偏好聚合为策略排名。此外提出一套协议，用于检验评测管线能否产生验证有效的策略结论，同时缓解传统真实评测的关键挑战。

Result: 在仿真与真实场景中的实验表明，R2S-Eval能够产生可靠且稳定的策略结论，与人类偏好达成一致，显著减少重复硬件操作负担，并能揭示二值成功率标签无法捕捉的行为质量差异。

Conclusion: R2S-Eval将机器人评估从人工计数成功率推进到自动化、统计稳定且质量感知的行为评估范式，为VLA等通用策略模型的评测提供了更高效可靠的方法。

Abstract: Evaluating robot manipulation policies is becoming increasingly important as generalist models, particularly vision-language-action (VLA) models, are deployed on physical robots. However, conventional real-world evaluation remains labor-intensive, unstable, and insufficiently informative. It requires repeated hardware trials, manual scene resets, and continuous operator monitoring, may produce different policy rankings across repeated evaluations, and primarily relies on success-rate metrics that provide limited information about execution quality. In contrast, humans assess robot performance by observing and comparing complete behaviors rather than relying solely on binary success outcomes. To this end, we propose R2S-Eval, an evaluation pipeline that combines real-to-sim calibration with vision-language model (VLM) preference evaluation. The real-to-sim component efficiently generates rollout videos in a simulator calibrated to the real-world evaluation setting, thereby reducing the need for repeated hardware trials. The VLM evaluator assesses the execution quality of rollout videos and produces pairwise preferences, which are subsequently aggregated into policy rankings. We further introduce a protocol to assess whether the proposed evaluation pipeline yields validated policy conclusions while mitigating the key challenges of conventional real-world evaluation. Experiments in both simulation and real-world settings demonstrate that R2S-Eval produces reliable and stable policy conclusions, achieves agreement with human preferences, substantially reduces repeated hardware-operation effort, and reveals behavior-quality differences that are not captured by binary success labels. In general, R2S-Eval advances robot evaluation from manual success counting toward automated, statistically stable, and quality-aware evaluation of robot behavior. Project page: https://r2s-eval.github.io.

</details>


### [340] [ARTiS: An Adaptive Robotic Gripper for Enhanced Tool Manipulation in Disassembly Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03362)
*Roman Mykhailyshyn,Domae Yukiyasu,Harada Kensuke*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ARtiS（自适应机器人工具夹持器），一种用于拆装系统的新型夹持器，它结合了软体夹持器的适应性、拟人手的灵巧性以及刚性机制的鲁棒性，通过软手掌与软指尖设计，利用手掌中的主动堵塞（active jamming）和指尖的fin-ray适应机制实现在多种情境下的牢固工具夹持，并凭借七自由度设计实现高手指灵巧度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在拆装过程中使用工具时，牢固夹持与灵巧操作是重大挑战。现有机器人夹持技术常受限于难以同时实现安全性、适应性、灵巧性与鲁棒性，尤其在需要快速自适应操作的装配与拆卸流程中效率与一致性难以保证。

Method: 设计ARtiS夹持器，融合软体夹持器适应性与刚性机制鲁棒性：手掌采用主动堵塞机制增强握持力，指尖采用fin-ray结构实现自适应包裹；结合七自由度手指设计，使指尖可朝向任意表面定向，同时支持自动化与协作任务场景。

Result: 使用一系列常规拆装工具进行了综合评价，考察了夹持器的顺应性、耐用性和功能多样性，验证了其综合性能。

Conclusion: ARtiS通过软硬结合的设计在工具夹持的牢固性、适应性与灵巧性之间取得平衡，为拆装系统中的机器人工具操作提供了一种新的解决方案，并为后续的硬件扩展与协作应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Grasping and holding tools while using them presents a considerable challenge not only for robots but also for humans. Such a challenge is particularly noticeable in processes involving assembly and disassembly, where efficiency and consistency depend on performing rapidly adaptive tasks. Nonetheless, contemporary robotic grasping technologies that can securely manipulate tools during operation frequently have significant constraints. In this paper, introduce ARTiS (Adaptive Robotic Tool Gripper in Disassembly Systems), a novel gripper that combines the adaptability of soft grippers, the dexterity of anthropomorphic hands, and the robustness of rigid mechanisms with a soft palm and fingertips. This unique combination makes it possible to hold tools securely in a variety of situations through using active jamming in the palm and fin-ray adaptation in fingertips. Furthermore, high finger dexterity is achieved through the seven degrees of freedom design, which enables the fingertips to orient to any surface, both for automated solutions and collaborative tasks. A comprehensive evaluation was conducted using a range of conventional disassembly tools to assess the gripper's compliance, durability, and functional versatility. More information, hardware instructions, and videos at https://romanmykhailyshyn.github.io/artis/

</details>


### [341] [Programming and execution of skill-based human-robot-crane collaborative tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03392)
*Taneli Lohi,Markku Suomalainen,Roope Mellanen,Tapio Heikkilä*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于CAD模型的编程与执行系统，用于参数化技能和技能监视器，采用基于事件通信的修改版行为树作为动态参数化执行控制结构，并以人-机器人-起重机协作技能作为测试案例进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高变化性的生产场景给机器人制造和室内物流带来挑战，需要新的敏捷性、灵活性和鲁棒性能力。

Method: 提出一种易于使用的基于CAD模型的编程与执行系统，用于参数化技能和技能监视器；采用修改版行为树作为动态参数化执行控制结构，该结构中仅使用基于事件的通信。

Result: 通过一个人-机器人-起重机协作技能测试案例进行展示：人类指导桥式起重机和机械臂，将起重机支撑的重物插入目标位置。

Conclusion: 该CAD模型驱动的参数化技能执行框架结合事件驱动的行为树控制结构，能够有效支持人机协作环境下的复杂操作任务。

Abstract: Highly varying production sets increasing challenges for robotic manufacturing and indoor logistics. New capabilities for agility, flexibility, and robustness are needed. Robot skills, integrating motions, tool operations, and sensor perceptions consistently provide an execution mechanism for a versatile set of tasks with varying parameters. In this paper, easy-to-use CAD-model based programming and execution system for parametrized skills and skill monitors is showcased. The execution control structure is dynamic and parametrized, based on a modified Behavior Tree, where only event based communication is used. A human-robot-crane collaborative skill is shown as a test example, where a human instructs an overhead crane and a manipulator in inserting a heavy object supported by the crane, and guided by the manipulator, into the goal.

</details>


### [342] [Air-Ground Collaborative Vision-and-Language Navigation via Shared Bird's-Eye Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03483)
*Shuning Zhang,Liang Li,Yunheng Wang,Tao Wang,Yihang Kang,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了AGC-VLN，这是首个用于空地协同视觉语言导航（VLN）的无需训练基线方法。其核心思路是将VLM语义推理与确定性几何执行解耦，通过UAV的全局鸟瞰视图渲染UGV位置及VLM锚定目标作为CAR/GOAL标记，形成共享地图，使UGV获得全局空间语义并借助冻结VLM规划沿路路径，同时UAV采用3D-SPF空间搜索升级算法定位并飞向目标。在CARLA-Air的Town10HD场景100个闭环回合中，联合成功率77.0%，比最弱单个体（UAV 50.0%）提升27.0%，超过最强单智能体基线Travel UAV（53.0%）24.0个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空地协同VLN将具备全局鸟瞰视角的无人机（UAV）与具备局部一视角的无人车（UGV）配对，但该场景尚未被充分探索：现有免训练方法只能解决单智能体任务且缺乏协作机制，CARLA-Air的近期评估显示五种先进VLA模型均未表现出稳定的协作行为，朴素的语义通信或双向耦合甚至使性能退化。因此亟需建立有效的空地协同导航框架。

Method: AGC-VLN将训练-free导航分解为VLM语义推理与确定性几何执行，暴露出协作接口：UAV在其全局视角中渲染UGV报告位姿与VLM锚定目标作为CAR/GOAL标记并附带距离标签，构成共享鸟瞰地图；UGV从中获取第一视角无法提供的全局空间上下文，利用冻结VLM规划沿路路径并在闭环控制下执行；同时UAV运行3D-SPF（SPF的空间搜索升级版）在下视视角中定位并飞向目标。通过"全局视角+沿路执行"的功能互补实现协同。

Result: 在CARLA-Air的Town10HD场景100个闭环回合中，AGC-VLN联合成功率达77.0%，相对最弱单个体UAV（50.0%）带来+27.0%的协作增益，并比最强公开单智能体基线Travel UAV（53.0%）高出24.0个百分点，证明了全局视角与沿路执行互补性的有效性。

Conclusion: AGC-VLN是首个免训练的空地协同VLN基线，通过将VLM语义推理与几何执行解耦并设计共享鸟瞰地图作为协作接口，实现了UAV全局感知与UGV沿路执行的有效互补，显著超越单智能体基线。该工作填补了空地协同VLN无稳定协作方案的空白，为后续研究提供了可复现的基准。

Abstract: Air-ground collaborative Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) pairs an unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) with a global bird's-eye view and an unmanned ground vehicle (UGV) with a local first-person view, yet the setting remains largely unexplored: existing training-free methods solve single-agent tasks but offer no collaboration mechanism, and a recent CARLA-Air evaluation found no stable cooperative behavior across five state-of-the-art VLA models; naive semantic communication or bidirectional coupling even degrades performance. We establish AGC-VLN (Air-Ground Collaborative VLN), the first training-free baseline for air-ground collaborative VLN. The key insight is that training-free methods decompose navigation into VLM-based semantic reasoning and deterministic geometric execution, exposing a collaboration interface: the UAV's global view, over which it renders the UGV's reported pose and the VLM-anchored target as CAR/GOAL markers with distance labels, yielding a shared bird's-eye map. From this map, the UGV acquires global spatial context its first-person view cannot provide, plans a road-following path with a frozen VLM, and executes it under closed-loop control; in parallel, the UAV runs 3D-SPF, a spatial-search upgrade of SPF that localizes the target in the downward view and flies toward it. On 100 closed-loop episodes in CARLA-Air's Town10HD scene, AGC-VLN reaches a 77.0% joint success rate, a collaboration gain of +27.0% over the weaker individual agent (the UAV, 50.0%), and exceeds the strongest published single-agent baseline (Travel UAV, 53.0%) by 24.0 points, stemming from the complementarity of the UAV's global view and the UGV's road-following execution. Project page: https://github.com/ZSN2024/AGC-VLN.

</details>


### [343] [BRIDGE: An Open-Source Humanoid Platform via Morphology-Control Co-Design for Physical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03497)
*Jianren Wang,Letian Qian,Zikai Wang,Weiwei Wu,Junjie Zong,Abhinav Gupta,Deepak Pathak*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种数据驱动的形态-控制协同设计框架，通过联合考虑运动学重定向保真度和动态跟踪性能的新指标来优化人形机器人形态，并实现了开源88cm高的人形平台Bridge，在基础运动、鲁棒平衡和高度动态操作上均优于基线人形机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人形机器人开发采用硬件设计与全身控制相分离的范式，导致系统次优，无法充分利用人类行为数据，也难以实现人类般的流畅性与敏捷性。作者旨在弥合从人类行为数据到通用形态人形机器人之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出一个数据驱动的形态-控制（morphology-control）协同设计框架，同时优化人形机器人的形态参数与全身控制策略；并引入一个新的量化指标，联合评估对人类运动的重定向保真度（kinematic retargeting fidelity）与动态跟踪性能（dynamic tracking performance）。

Result: 框架在所有指标上均取得SOTA性能，优于Bumi、K1和Toddlerbot等基线人形平台。实际设计并开源了Bridge——一个88cm高的人形机器人平台及其配套控制策略，展示了卓越的人类运动数据捕获保真度，并在基础运动、鲁棒平衡和高动态操作任务中表现出色。

Conclusion: 独立的硬件设计与控制还不足以开发真正类人的人形机器人，形态与控制需要协同优化。Bridge平台证明了这种数据驱动的协同设计路径的可行性，其开源发布为社区提供了进一步研究的基础。

Abstract: Developing humanoid robots capable of leveraging human behavioral data is essential for general-purpose embodiment, yet conventional development remains bottlenecked by a decoupled paradigm that isolates hardware design from whole-body control. This approach leads to suboptimal systems that compromise human-like fluidity and agility. To bridge this gap, we introduce a data-driven morphology-control co-design framework that optimizes humanoid morphology for human-like movement. To quantify morphological fidelity, we also introduce a novel metric that jointly considers kinematic retargeting fidelity to human motion and dynamic tracking performance. Our framework achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance across all metrics compared to baseline humanoids (Bumi, K1, and Toddlerbot). Finally, we realize this design in Bridge, an open-source, 88cm-tall humanoid platform released alongside its control policy. We demonstrate that Bridge captures human motion data with superior fidelity, exhibiting exceptional performance across foundational locomotion, robust balance, and highly dynamic maneuvers. Videos and open-source materials: https://sites.google.com/view/bridgerobot.

</details>


### [344] [TRaIL-Odom: Tightly Coupled Continuous Time Radar-IMU-LiDAR Odometry with Adaptive Doppler Weighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03561)
*Chiyun Noh,Turcan Tuna,William Talbot,Marco Hutter,Laurent Kneip,Ayoung Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TRaIL-Odom框架，在紧耦合雷达-IMU-LiDAR里程计系统中引入两种退变感知的多普勒加权模块（逐点雷达重加权与逐扫描雷达增益调度），根据LiDAR几何退变方向自适应调整雷达贡献。在13个序列上达到SOTA性能，消融实验显示组合两种自适应加权模块可将RMSE ATE和RTE分别降低86.0%和78.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有雷达-激光雷达融合方法使用固定残差权重，但雷达Doppler和LiDAR几何信息的相关性随扫描和方向变化。固定权重会导致统一的雷达加权，在平移方向上错误分配Doppler信息，无法适应几何退化场景。

Method: 提出了两种退变感知的多普勒重加权模块：(1) 逐点雷达重加权——从LiDAR几何中识别弱平移方向，基于单个雷达Doppler约束与弱子空间的对齐程度进行重加权；(2) 逐扫描雷达增益调度——利用LiDAR几何各向异性调节整体雷达贡献，当LiDAR可观测性差时增强雷达权重，当LiDAR约束可靠时抑制雷达权重。两者集成于紧耦合的Radar-IMU-LiDAR里程计框架中。

Result: 在13个评估序列上取得SOTA综合性能，在几何退化场景中优势明显。在三个退化序列的消融实验中，组合两种自适应加权模块相对固定权重基线，RMSE ATE下降86.0%，RTE下降78.5%。代码和数据集已开源。

Conclusion: 通过方向感知和扫描感知的自适应Doppler加权，有效解决了雷达-LiDAR融合中固定权重的缺陷，特别是在LiDAR几何退化场景下显著提升里程计精度，验证了自适应权重分配策略的有效性。

Abstract: Existing radar-LiDAR fusion methods rely on fixed residual weights, even though the informativeness of radar Doppler and LiDAR geometry is scan- and direction-dependent, leading to uniform radar weighting that misallocates Doppler information across translational directions. To address this limitation, we propose two degeneracy-aware Doppler reweighting modules within a tightly coupled Radar-IMU-LiDAR odometry framework: per-point radar reweighting and scan-wise radar gain scheduling. Since geometric degeneracy is directional, we first identify weak translational directions from the LiDAR geometry and reweight individual radar Doppler constraints based on their alignment with the weak subspace. We further adjust the overall radar contribution using LiDAR geometric anisotropy such that radar is emphasized when LiDAR observability is poor and suppressed when LiDAR constraints are already reliable. Across 13 evaluated sequences, TRaIL-Odom achieves state-of-the-art overall performance, with clear advantages in geometrically degenerate scenes. In ablation experiments on three degenerate sequences, combining the two adaptive weighting modules reduces RMSE ATE and RTE by 86.0% and 78.5% relative to the fixed-weight baseline. We make our code and an accompanying dataset publicly available at https://github.com/ChiyunNoh/TRaIL-Odom.

</details>


### [345] [Toward Physically Grounded JEPA World Models for Goal-Conditioned Robotic Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03565)
*Muyuan Liu,Yue Huang,Zheng Liang,Xiang Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种端到端的JEPA世界模型，在潜在状态预测的基础上同时引入逆动力学（IDM）与状态对齐（SA）约束，使潜在表征保留更多与机器人控制相关的信息。在四个基准任务上，模型在TwoRoom（100%）、PushT（98%）和OGBench-Cube（87%）上取得最高成功率，并在Reacher上表现与LeWorldModel相当。消融实验显示，加入状态对齐能一致性地提升规划成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有action-conditioned JEPA世界模型通过重建无目标像素的潜在预测来实现规划，但仅靠潜在预测并未显式鼓励表征保留与机器人控制相关的信息，导致潜在状态可能缺失对规划至关重要的控制线索。

Method: 在JEPA世界模型的潜在预测之上，联合引入两个辅助目标：(1) 逆动力学（IDM），通过预测产生相邻潜状态的动作来抑制潜空间崩塌并使转换信息与动作相关联；(2) 状态对齐（SA），将连续潜表征与对应的物理构型与运动量对齐，赋予表征物理语义。三者以端到端方式联合训练。

Result: 在TwoRoom、PushT、OGBench-Cube上分别取得100%、98%、87%的成功率（均超过基线），在Reacher上与LeWorldModel性能相当。消融显示SA在四个任务上均优于仅用IDM。尽管LeWorldModel在OGBench-Cube上的straightening平均值更高，但其转换能量集中在显著更低维的子空间中；所提出模型的有效转换维度更高，规划性能更强。

Conclusion: 状态对齐是逆动力学的有效补充，能够提高潜在转换的有效维度、保留更丰富的控制相关信息，从而提升基于JEPA的机器人规划的鲁棒性与成功率。

Abstract: Action-conditioned JEPA world models enable planning toward visually specified goals without reconstructing future pixels, yet latent prediction alone does not explicitly encourage the learned representations to retain information relevant to robotic control. We introduce an end-to-end JEPA world model that augments latent prediction with inverse dynamics (IDM) and state alignment (SA). While inverse dynamics discourages latent collapse and makes latent transitions informative of the actions that produced them, state alignment grounds consecutive representations in their associated physical configuration and motion. Across four benchmark tasks, our model attains the highest success rates on TwoRoom (100%), PushT (98%), and OGBench-Cube (87%), while performing comparably to LeWorldModel on Reacher. Our ablation further shows that adding state alignment consistently improves planning success over IDM alone across all four tasks. Although LeWorldModel, our primary baseline, attains higher average straightening on OGBench-Cube, transition-subspace analysis shows that its transition energy is concentrated in a substantially lower-dimensional subspace. Our state-aligned model exhibits a higher effective transition dimension than LeWorldModel and improves planning over IDM alone, supporting state alignment as an effective complement to inverse dynamics for robotic planning.

</details>


### [346] [Scaling Bimanual Household Manipulation from 1,500 hours of Demonstrations to On-Policy Corrections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03591)
*Jiafeng Xu,Qi Li,Yan Shen,Yiyu Ren,Travis Davies,Shaowen He,Ze Wang,Yifan Yang,Ran Cheng,Hao Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文发布了1500小时的双臂操作演示数据集，并基于此训练了XR-2视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型。通过高吞吐量数据流水线和多阶段训练范式，XR-2在操作任务上表现出色，并且随着演示数据量和DAgger矫正数据的增加，任务成功率持续稳定提升，显示出清晰的扩展趋势。数据集已开源以支持可复现的双臂机器人操作学习研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双臂机器人操作领域缺乏高质量、大规模的人类演示数据，这成为训练通用鲁棒双臂操作策略的主要瓶颈。

Method: 1) 构建1500小时覆盖日常家务任务的双臂操作演示数据集；2) 设计高吞吐量数据采集流水线；3) 采用精心设计的多阶段训练范式训���XR-2 VLA模型；4) 系统性地研究两个扩展维度：专家演示数据量的变化和基于DAgger真实人类实时干预矫正数据的后训练。

Result: XR-2在系统实验中展现出强大的操作性能，同时保持良好的训练效率和高数据利用率；在两个扩展轴上的实验均显示任务成功率随数据量增加而持续稳定提升，呈现清晰一致的扩展趋势。

Conclusion: 实验结果验证了XR-2的学习能力以及所发布数据集良好的可扩展特性，并将数据集开源以促进双臂机器人操作学习的可复现研究。

Abstract: Learning generalist policies for robust bimanual manipulation is bottlenecked by the scarcity of high quality large scale human demonstration data. In this work, we release 1,500 hours of diverse bimanual manipulation demonstrations covering everyday household tasks, and use this comprehensive corpus to train XR-2, a powerful vision-language-action (VLA) model. Enabled by a purpose built high throughput data pipeline and a carefully designed multi stage training paradigm, XR-2 attains strong manipulation performance in our systematic experiments while retaining favorable training efficiency and high data utilization. We further study two critical scaling axes: varying the amount of expert demonstration data, and post training on DAgger correction data from real time human interventions. In both settings, task success rate improves steadily over the data ranges we probe, exhibiting a clear consistent scaling trend at our current data scale. These results validate both the learning capacity of XR-2 and the promising scaling properties of the released dataset, which we open source to support reproducible research on bimanual robot manipulation learning.

</details>


### [347] [FailBench: How Reliable are VLMs at Judging Robot Task Success?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03611)
*Zaruhi Navasardyan,Tatul Danielyan,Hrant Davtyan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FailBench基准，用于评估VLM在机器人操作失败检测中的跨域泛化能力，包含2,197个操作尝试（来自14个公开数据源）。评估13种VLM检测器后发现最佳模型平均平衡准确率仅0.77，专门微调的失败检测模型反而普遍不如通用VLM及其实验前的预训练基线；模型在依赖可见物体运动的任务上接近饱和，但在接触密集型装配任务上退化至接近随机水平；错误分析揭示模型在模糊证据下存在系统性偏向预测成功的倾向；通过输入层空间定位裁剪可将最佳检测器提升2.4个百分点而无需额外训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM评估机器人操作结果的基准缺乏跨域泛化的充分证据，需要有更全面、更具挑战性的基准来系统评估VLM在真实多样场景下的机器人失败检测能力。

Method: 构建FailBench数据集，包含来自14个公开数据源（12个真实世界、2个模拟）的2,197个操作尝试，其中75%的失败自然发生，6个真实世界来源来自非失败检测数据集。评估13种VLM检测器，并进行错误分析研究预测失败模式，同时测试输入层空间定位与裁剪干预。

Result: 最佳模型平均平衡准确率仅0.77；专门微调的模型表现不及通用VLM；模型在可观察物体运动的任务上接近饱和但在接触密集的装配任务上低于0.60平衡准确率；模型在模糊证据下系统性偏向预测成功；空间定位裁剪干预提升最佳检测器2.4个百分点。

Conclusion: 当前VLM在机器人失败检测的跨域泛化能力仍然有限，尤其在需要细粒度接触推断的装配类任务上表现接近随机；提示推理并未克服模型的乐观偏差，输入层面的空间定位干预是一种有效且无需额外训练的改进方向。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly used to evaluate robot manipulation outcomes, but existing benchmarks offer limited evidence of cross-domain generalization. We introduce FailBench, a benchmark for robot failure detection comprising 2,197 manipulation attempts across 14 public sources (12 real-world, 2 simulated). In FailBench, 75% of failures occur naturally, and six real-world sources come from non-failure-detection datasets. Evaluating 13 VLM-based detectors, we find the best model achieves only 0.77 mean balanced accuracy. Notably, models fine-tuned for failure detection consistently underperform general-purpose VLMs and their own pretrained baselines. Performance depends heavily on required visual evidence: models approach saturation when outcomes depend on observable object motion, but degrade to near-chance (<0.60 balanced accuracy) on contact-intensive assembly tasks. Error analysis reveals a systematic bias toward predicting success under ambiguous evidence, which persists even with increased reasoning effort. Finally, we show that input-level intervention--spatially localizing and cropping outcome-relevant regions--improves the top detector by 2.4 percentage points without extra training.

</details>


### [348] [QLAUN: A Research-Oriented, Robust, Agile, Modular, and Affordable Torque-Controlled Quadruped Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03623)
*Mohamad S. Moudallal,Noel J. Maalouf*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种名为QLAUN Bot的四足机器人平台，其设计目标是在保持低成本和高可及性的同时，实现鲁棒性与敏捷性。该机器人采用3D打印技术制造，具备模块化可更换腿部设计和创新的无电子器件腿部设计策略，旨在推动黎巴嫩及中东北非地区大学和研究机构的机器人研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为黎巴嫩和中东北非地区的大学及研究机构提供研究导向、成本效益高的四足机器人研究平台，以降低研究门槛，使更广泛的学术群体能够参与腿式机器人运动研究。该地区在此领域的研究基础设施较为薄弱，缺乏经济可及的高性能平台。

Method: 采用几乎完全3D打印的制造方式（主体使用PLA材料），设计了一个15 kg、12自由度（每腿三个）的四足机器人。其核心创新包括：(1) 创新的无电子器件腿部设计策略；(2) 模块化且可互换的腿部结构，髋关节和膝关节执行器解耦以提高模块性；(3) 每腿配备完全3D打印的准直驱（QDD）执行器，由无刷直流电机、低减速比齿轮箱及皮带传动系统构成以显著提升关节扭矩；(4) 宽运动范围的关节设计，包括连续的髋关节屈伸关节；(5) 使用TPU-95A打印的柔性足端，用于缓冲冲击和应对地形不确定性。

Result: 文章提出了一种扭矩控制的全3D打印四足机器人平台，验证了利用低价零部件和增材制造技术构建具备QDD驱动、宽关节运动范围和柔性足端的敏捷鲁棒腿式机器人的可行性，为腿式机器人运动控制研究提供了经济实用的实验平台。

Conclusion: QLAUN旨在向学术界展示一种融合模块化设计、3D打印制造和QDD驱动理念的新型研究平台，通过强调成本效益和可维护性，为资源有限的大学和研究机构提供高性能的四足机器人研究工具，有望促进该地区腿式机器人研究的发展。

Abstract: QLAUN Bot (Quad-Legged Adaptive Unmanned Navigator Robot) is a torque-controlled quadruped robot that is research-oriented, cost-effective, and aimed at achieving simultaneous robustness and agility while being completely 3D-printed. It is a quadruped robot that is aimed at empowering robotics research at universities and research institutes in Lebanon and the MENA region. Using a novel electronics-free leg design strategy, we present a modular robot with interchangeable and easily replaceable legs. The 15 kg robot possesses 12 DoF (Degrees-of-Freedom) with three per leg, each paired with a completely 3D-printed Quasi-Direct Drive (QDD) actuator that consists of a brushless DC motor and a low-ratio gearbox transmission that is connected to a belt transmission system for significantly increasing the torque outputs at the joints. We present legs that have decoupled hip and knee actuators to improve the overall modularity of the robot. QLAUN is almost completely 3D-printed using polylactic acid (PLA) and assembled using off-the-shelf parts to create a robust, agile, and affordable robot for legged robot locomotion research. The legs possess joints with wide ranges of motion, including a continuous hip flexion-extension joint. A compliant foot, printed using TPU-95A is also implemented for alleviating hard impacts and handling terrain uncertainties. This extended abstract aims to introduce QLAUN, a novel platform for robotics research, emphasizing the design concepts and principles that underpin its development to the academic and research communities in the field of robotics.

</details>


### [349] [Local Path Planning and Obstacle Avoidance for an Omnicopter Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03630)
*Mikolaj Helinski,Spilios Theodoulis,Mahmoud Hamandi,Abdullah Mohamed Ali,Anthony Tzes,Marija Popovic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向全向多旋翼（omnicopter）的六自由度动态窗口法（6D-DWA），通过局部体素化地图、球体几何近似和自适应速度采样实现实时局部规划与避障，并引入上下文感知的"敏捷模式"在线调整评分权重以提升对未知障碍物的响应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主无人机在杂乱环境中运行时，全局规划器（如RRT*）无法直接以控制速率部署，需要一种能够在六自由度空间内实时运行、同时兼顾全局路径跟踪与局部避障能力的规划方法。

Method: 将经典动态窗口法（DWA）从3自由度扩展到六自由度（6D-DWA），包含三大关键技术：(i)局部地图体素化以支持实时碰撞检测；(ii)采用紧凑的球体近似无人机几何外形；(iii)在六维搜索空间内进行自适应速度采样。此外提出"敏捷模式"（Agile Mode），根据上下文在线调整评分权重，在目标进度、安全距离和朝向约束之间动态权衡，以增强对未知障碍物的规避反应。

Result: 规划器能够在0.2s控制周期内稳定运行；对密集路点全局路径的跟踪平均横向误差小于0.1m、平均航向误差为13°；在静态环境中可成功避障。针对未知障碍物，敏捷模式对偏置障碍物的避障成功率达79.3%，对居中障碍物成功率为41.4%，兼顾了自适应加权的有效性与高度受限几何空间下的剩余局限。

Conclusion: 6D-DWA结合敏捷模式在计算效率和未知障碍物响应方面表现良好，能够满足全向多旋翼在杂乱环境中的实时局部规划需求；但在高度受限几何场景（如居中障碍物）下仍有待改进的空间。

Abstract: Autonomous unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) increasingly operate in cluttered environments where global planners such as RRT* are not directly deployable at control rates. This paper presents a real-time local planning and obstacle avoidance module for an omnidirectional multirotor (omnicopter) by extending the Dynamic Window Approach to six degrees of freedom (6D-DWA). Our method achieves real-time feasibility through (i) local-map voxelisation, (ii) a compact sphere-based approximation of the vehicle geometry, and (iii) adaptive velocity sampling in the 6D search space. To improve reactivity to unknown obstacles, we introduce a context-aware "Agile Mode" that adjusts scoring weights online to trade-off between goal progress, clearance, and heading/facing constraints during evasive manoeuvres. We evaluate our approach in simulation across computational stress tests, dense-waypoint path tracking, and static/unknown obstacle scenarios. Our planner runs consistently within a 0.2s control loop, tracks waypoint-dense global paths with < 0.1m average cross-track error and 13deg average heading error, and avoids collisions in static environments. For unknown obstacle avoidance, Agile Mode achieves 79.3% success for an off-centre obstacle and 41.4% for a centred obstacle, highlighting both the effectiveness of adaptive weighting and remaining limitations in highly constrained geometries.

</details>


### [350] [WISE: World-model-guided Imagination Scheduling for Efficient Post-training of Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03681)
*Chenhao Zhang,Hanyu Zhao,Hang Cheng,Tengfei Pan,Long Zeng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WISE是一个统一的世界模型引导想象调度框架，用于视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略的高效后训练。它通过选择性调用世界模型想象、有界多视角展开、基于进展与完成信号的候选评估，以及从真实交互上下文精炼动作，在不依赖昂贵示范或真实世界探索的前提下提升策略性能，且在两种VLA基座上均取得一致改进，并减少约80%的GPU计算时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略的后训练通常依赖于两类昂贵途径：需要专家示范的监督微调，或需要真实世界探索的强化学习。世界模型提供了一种有前景的替代方案，但对VLA后训练而言，仅具备准确预测能力是不够的——想象必须在有用之处被调度、在可靠的视界内受限，并能转化为可信的策略监督信号。在机器人操作任务中，想象的价值随执行阶段显著变化，而延长的展开会累积预测误差并引入不可靠的学习信号。

Method: WISE框架包含三个关键机制：(1) 在交互相关状态上选择性调用世界模型想象；(2) 执行有界的多视角展开以避免误差累积；(3) 利用进展与完成信号评估候选未来，并使用其相对结果来精炼由真实交互上下文产生的动作。

Result: 在π_0和π_{0.5}两种VLA基座上，WISE在多种操作任务中均实现了一致的性能改进，同时相比全展开想象方式将GPU计算时间减少约80%。真实世界评估进一步表明其在多种分布偏移下具有显著的鲁棒性和泛化性提升。

Conclusion: WISE证明了世界模型想象的价值在于"何时用"和"如何用"而非仅"能用"——通过合理的调度、约束和信号转换机制，世界模型可以成为VLA策略高效后训练中省力且可靠的替代方案，既能大幅降低计算成本，又能提升策略在真实世界中的鲁棒性与泛化能力。

Abstract: Post-training VLA policies typically rely on supervised fine-tuning with costly expert demonstrations or reinforcement learning with expensive and potentially unstable real-world exploration. World models offer a promising alternative by evaluating candidate behaviors through imagined futures, yet effective post-training requires more than accurate prediction: imagination must be scheduled where it is useful, bounded within reliable horizons, and translated into trustworthy policy supervision. In robotic manipulation, the value of imagination varies substantially across execution stages, while extended rollouts can accumulate prediction errors and introduce unreliable learning signals. We introduce WISE (World-model-guided Imagination Scheduling for Efficient Post-training of Vision-Language-Action Models), a unified framework that coordinates when and how world-model imagination is used during policy refinement. WISE selectively invokes imagination at interaction-relevant states, performs bounded multi-view rollouts, evaluates candidate futures using progress and completion signals, and uses their relative outcomes to refine actions generated from real interaction contexts. Extensive experiments with both $π_0$ and $π_{0.5}$ demonstrate consistent improvements across diverse manipulation tasks while reducing GPU computation time by approximately 80% compared with full imagination. Real-world evaluations further show substantial gains in robustness and generalization under diverse real-world distribution shifts.

</details>


### [351] [Predictive Zonotope Reduction: Precise Runtime Monitoring under Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03699)
*Vladimir Krsmanovic,Florian Kohn,Bernd Finkbeiner,Milan Simovic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出预测性Zonotope缩减（PZR）方法，将缩减器选择建模为最优控制问题，通过波束搜索模型预测控制求解，并利用策略蒸馏得到小规模神经策略以提升执行速度，从而在资源受限的实时系统中实现不确定性感知的运行监控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在物理环境中基于不确定的传感器测量进行控制决策，可能导致不安全或次优行为。运行时监控器需要用Zonotope表示这种不确定性，但持续融入新测量会导致Zonotope阶数无限增长，必须定期进行过近似缩减。现有方法全程使用固定缩减方法，然而最优缩减方法的选择依赖于当前状态，静态策略会导致精度损失和更高的误报率。

Method: PZR将缩减器选择问题形式化为最优控制问题，采用波束搜索模型预测控制（MPC）求解；随后通过策略蒸馏将MPC策略压缩为小型神经策略，在保持性能的同时大幅提升执行速度。该方法实现在RLola运行时监控框架中。

Result: 在MuJoCo仿真的5自由度机械臂（传感器不确定性按ISO 5725建模）上的实验表明，与静态缩减策略相比，动态缩减显著降低了运行时监控的误报率。在Raspberry Pi 5上的实验验证了该方法的可行性。

Conclusion: 动态选择Zonotope缩减方法相比于静态固定策略能显著降低误报率，且通过策略蒸馏实现的高效神经策略能够满足资源受限实时系统的执行需求，为不确定性感知的运行时监控提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Robots operating in physical environments make control decisions based on uncertain sensor measurements, which can lead to unsafe or suboptimal actions. Runtime monitors that check their behavior against safety specifications must represent this uncertainty soundly. Zonotopes are a widely used representation, but continuously incorporating new measurements grows their order unboundedly, so monitors must periodically apply an over-approximating reduction. The choice of the reduction method substantially affects the zonotope's precision, yet existing approaches typically utilize a fixed method throughout the run, even though the optimal choice depends on the current state. This paper presents a Predictive Zonotope Reduction (PZR) approach, which frames reducer selection as an optimal control problem and solves it using beam-search model predictive control. Policy distillation into a small neural policy further provides substantially higher execution speed than model predictive control while maintaining improved performance, enabling uncertainty-aware runtime monitoring on resource-constrained real-time systems. We implement our approach in the RLola runtime monitoring framework and evaluate it on a 5-degree-of-freedom robotic arm simulated in MuJoCo, with sensor uncertainty modeled according to ISO 5725. Experiments on a Raspberry Pi 5 show that dynamic reduction significantly lowers false-positive rates in monitoring compared with static reduction strategies.

</details>


### [352] [Toward an~Integrated Cognitive--Ergonomic Architecture for~Human--Machine Interaction: Combining Cognitive Models with~Human Factors Ergonomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03704)
*Antoine Lenat,Olivier Cheminat,Damien Chablat,Camilo Charron*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种集成方法，将认知架构的理论基础（SOAR、ACT-R、LIDA、COCOM）与人为因素工效学（HFE）原理相结合，综合出一种面向动态环境中人机交互（HMI）的定制化架构，并在工业机器人场景中进行实证验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弥合认知理论与应用系统设计之间的鸿沟，解决动态环境中人机交互的复杂性，特别是工业机器人应用中操作员专业知识、规范知识与实时反馈环路的关键需求。

Method: 对SOAR、ACT-R、LIDA和COCOM四种成熟认知模型进行比较分析，将认知架构理论融入人为因素工效学框架，综合出定制的认知架构来建模决策制定、技能习得和适应性行为机制；以工业机器人应用为实证背景进行验证。

Result: 所提出的架构增强了人机交互系统的认知对齐性，提供了在高风险环境中设计智能、以人为本的界面的可扩展方法，并在工业机器人场景中获得了实证支持。

Conclusion: 该工作同时推进了人类能力建模的理论理解，以及自适应、工效学优化系统的实践实现。

Abstract: This paper presents an integrated approach to modeling human competencies by combining the theoretical foundations of cognitive architectures with principles from Human Factors Ergonomics (HFE). Through a comparative analysis of established cognitive models-SOAR, ACT-R, LIDA, and COCOM-we synthesize a tailored architecture designed to address the complexities of human-machine interaction (HMI) in dynamic environments. By contextualizing this model within ergonomic frameworks, we elucidate the mechanisms underlying decision-making, skill acquisition, and adaptive behavior, bridging the gap between cognitive theory and applied system design. Our framework is empirically grounded in industrial robotics applications, where operator expertise, normative knowledge, and real-time feedback loops are critical. The proposed architecture not only enhances the cognitive alignment of HMI systems but also provides a scalable methodology for designing intelligent, human-centered interfaces in high-stakes environments. This work advances both the theoretical understanding of human competencies and the practical implementation of adaptive, ergonomically optimized systems.

</details>


### [353] [MINERVA: How Small Can a Manipulation Policy Be and Still Solve LIBERO?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03715)
*Kohei Sendai,Tatsuya Matsushima,Yusuke Iwasawa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MINERVA系列极小规模视觉运动策略，仅有0.54M参数即可在LIBERO基准上达到95.1%的平均成功率（仅比π0.5低2.4个百分点，但参数少7700倍），揭示LIBERO基准的任务特异性容量下限远低于现有大规模VLA模型的容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大量亿级参数VLA模型主导了LIBERO操作基准，但该基准实际所需的模型容量下限始终不明确。理解这一任务特定的容量下限对于容量感知的设计和部署高效的机器人策略至关重要。

Method: 构建MINERVA家族——一系列刻意设计的小型视觉运动策略，从0.54M参数规模开始，进行广泛的架构、训练和推理消融实验。具体包括：测量不同参数规模下的成功率饱和点与崩溃点；对比flow matching与L1回归的差异；设计task-ID排列探针测试指令条件化是否仅依赖于记忆任务映射；在LIBERO-90和LIBERO-Plus上验证泛化性；在笔记本电脑CPU上进行推理速度测试。

Result: 0.54M参数策略在LIBERO四个标准套件共2000次rollout中达到95.1%平均成功率；性能在约1M参数处饱和、低于0.25M时崩溃；消融发现仅action-chunk长度和视觉容量对性能有显著影响（超过训练种子±1点带宽）；flow matching相比直接L1回归无优势且GPU推理慢3.8倍；task-ID置换探测将成功率降至接近随机水平，证明标准LIBERO条件化主要是在记忆任务；LIBERO-90上达到94.6%，但LIBERO-Plus扰动下降至46-56%；CPU推理每chunk仅5-9ms，比SmolVLA快113倍、比π0.5快1400倍。

Conclusion: 该研究首次对LIBERO基准的任务特异性容量下限给出了经验估计：不到0.54M参数的策略即可胜任该基准，远低于现有大规模VLA模型所投入的容量。这凸显了容量感知设计与蒸馏在部署高效机器人策略中的必要性，也揭示了LIBERO标准套件存在任务记忆偏差、鲁棒性不足等问题。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models with billions of parameters now dominate the LIBERO manipulation benchmark, but the model capacity actually required by the benchmark remains unclear. We introduce MINERVA (MINimal Efficient Robotic Vision-Action policy), a family of deliberately compact visuomotor policies designed to measure this task-specific capacity floor. A 0.54M-parameter policy achieves 95.1% average success over 2,000 rollouts on the four standard LIBERO suites, only 2.4 points below the reported LeRobot $π_{0.5}$ result despite using 7,700$\times$ fewer parameters. Performance saturates near 1M parameters and collapses below 0.25M. Across broad architectural, training, and inference sweeps, only action-chunk length and vision capacity consistently exceed a $\pm$1-point training-seed band. Flow matching provides no detectable advantage over direct L1 regression across three seeds, while regression is up to 3.8$\times$ faster on GPU. A task-ID permutation probe shows that standard LIBERO instruction conditioning primarily selects among memorized tasks: changing only the task-ID mapping reduces success to near chance. The same recipe achieves 94.6% success across 89 LIBERO-90 tasks, while LIBERO-Plus perturbations reduce performance to 46--56%, with near-zero robustness to photometric shifts. The 0.54M policy replans every control step in 5--9 ms per chunk on a laptop CPU, 113$\times$ faster than SmolVLA and 1,400$\times$ faster than $π_{0.5}$, without a GPU. These results establish a first empirical estimate of LIBERO's task-specific capacity floor and motivate capacity-aware design and distillation for deployment-efficient robot policies.

</details>


### [354] [RoughSense: Lightweight Terrain-Induced Rover Vibration Prediction Using Point Clouds and IMU Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03720)
*Gabriel Manuel Garcia,Stephanie Aravecchia,Miguel Angel Olivares-Mendez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于LiDAR点云和IMU测量的轻量级实时振动感知可通行性建图方法，通过RANSAC从地形几何估计初始振动代理，并利用递推最小二乘在线校正，使其适应漫游车的实际响应。方法在月球模拟环境、户外场地和地下矿井中进行了评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主空间导航（尤其是地下环境）需要可靠的地形评估以保证安全操作，但面临通信受限、计算资源有限和功率预算紧张等挑战。现有方法缺乏对地形可通行性中振动因素的轻量级实时评估手段。

Method: 首先利用SLAM算法生成的局部点云块，通过RANSAC拟合估计地形几何作为初始振动代理；并行地，IMU提供漫游车行驶过程中的局部振动观测；随后使用递推最小二乘（RLS）在线校正基于点云的振动预测，使几何估计适应实测的漫游车响应。

Result: 该方法在三种环境中进行了评估：月球模拟环境、户外野外场地以及地下矿井，验证了其在多样地形条件下的振动感知可通行性建图能力。

Conclusion: 所提出的轻量级方法能够在资源受限的地下环境中实现实时的振动感知可通行性建图，通过将几何估计与实际测量在线融合，提高了地形评估的可靠性。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation in space requires reliable terrain assessment for safe operations, especially in underground environments with limited communication, computing resources, and power budget. This paper presents a lightweight method for real-time vibration-aware traversability mapping using a Light Detecting And Ranging (LiDAR) point cloud and Inertial Measurement Unit (IMU) measurements. An initial vibration proxy is estimated from terrain geometry by applying Random sample consensus (RANSAC) to local point-cloud patches produced by a Simultaneous Localisation And Mapping (SLAM) algorithm. In parallel, the IMU provides local observations of the vibration experienced by the rover during traversal. The point-cloud-based prediction is then corrected online using Recursive Least Squares, allowing the system to adapt the geometric estimate to the measured rover response. The approach is evaluated in a lunar analogue environment, an outdoor field, and an underground mine.

</details>


### [355] [A Multi-Vine Soft Robot Enabling Accessible Working Channel and Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03758)
*Reza Kashef,Cem Suulker,Mohammad Sheikh Sofla,Kaspar Althoefer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多藤蔓（multi-vine）软体外翻机器人架构，将两个藤蔓机器人通过软安装尖端耦合到外部集成的工作通道上，通过独立的藤蔓驱动器实现主动尖端转向，同时推进工作通道而不将其嵌入藤蔓本体。实验证明该架构可在生长过程中实现近90度的锐利转向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的软体外翻（藤蔓）机器人在复杂环境如人体结肠中进行导航时，被动生长受限于锐利弯曲路径（如乙状结肠），需要主动转向；同时许多应用需要载荷输送（如传感器和微型手术工具）。已有将工作通道嵌入藤蔓本体的方案会限制工具尺寸、引入摩擦并限制环境可达性，因此需要一种既能主动转向又能灵活输送工具的新架构。

Method: 提出多藤蔓架构：将两个藤蔓机器人通过软安装尖端（soft mounting tips）耦合到一个外部集成的工作通道上。通过两个藤蔓的独立驱动实现主动尖端转向，同时推进工作通道前进，工作通道不被嵌入藤蔓本体内部。

Result: 实验演示了该架构在生长过程中能够实现近90度的锐利转向，表明其具有在医疗和非医疗应用中使用的潜力。

Conclusion: 该多藤蔓架构通过解耦工作通道与藤蔓本体，同时利用双藤蔓独立驱动实现主动转向，为微型手术工具递送和复杂环境导航提供了新的可行方案，适用于多种医疗和非医疗场景。

Abstract: Soft eversion robots, also known as vine robots, have attracted growing interest for navigation and inspection tasks, including minimally invasive medical applications [1]. A vine robot consists of a thin, flexible, inextensible tube folded inward that everts and grows forward when pressurized. This tip-growth enables navigation with minimal friction, making vine robots well suited for complex environments such as the human colon [2]. While their inherent softness allows passive conforma- tion to curved pathways in confined spaces, navigation performance strongly depends on environmental inter- actions, including contact angle and the length of un- constrained deployed material [3], [4]. Sharp directional changes, such as those in the sigmoid colon, often limit passive growth and necessitate active steering. Existing solutions include distributed artificial muscles [5] or dedicated tip-based steering mechanisms [6]. In addition, many applications require payload delivery, such as sensors and tools [7], [8]. Within the ERC Synergy project EndoTheranostics, this motivates the development of vine robots capable of delivering micro- surgical tools during growth. Prior work has integrated working channels within the vine body [8], [9], but these approaches constrain tool size, introduce friction, and limit access to the environment to the robot tip. In this work, we propose a multi-vine architecture in which two vine robots are coupled to an externally integrated working channel via soft mounting tips [10]. Independent vine actuation enables active tip steering while advancing the working channel without embed- ding it within the vine bodies Figure 1. Experiments demonstrate sharp steering of nearly 90 degrees during growth, highlighting the potential of this architecture for versatile medical and non-medical applications.

</details>


### [356] [Virtual Testing of Automated Driving Systems through Credible Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03760)
*Riccardo Dona,Espedito Rusciano,Biagio Ciuffo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于风险的仿真工具链可信度评估框架，用于自动驾驶系统（ADS）安全评估中的虚拟测试认可，借鉴NASA STD-7009方法，将可信度要求与预期用途及决策安全关键性挂钩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ADS复杂性和ODD规模使得纯物理测试不可行，虚拟测试在审批阶段广泛应用，而现有ADS领域的VT认可方法依赖纯验证实践，在面对复杂多工具仿真环境时扩展性差，需要更有效的可信度评估框架。

Method: 提出基于风险的仿真工具链可信度评估框架，扩展传统V&V方法，将可信度要求与仿真输出的预期用途及审批流程中决策安全关键性显式关联；提供覆盖工具链管理、建模假设与局限、验证、确认和敏感性分析的生命周期导向评估方案，并按比例定义可信度接受阈值，区分探索性安全分析、部分决策支持及物理测试替代等不同场景。

Result: 该框架在ADS领域得到演示验证，并可直接适用于道路安全及以虚拟测试为核心的安全评估与监管决策仿真研究。

Conclusion: 相比传统validation-only实践，所提基于风险的可信度框架能更好地应对复杂多工具仿真环境，通过差异化接受阈值实现按用途区分的可信度要求，为ADS审批阶段虚拟测试认可提供了更可扩展的方案。

Abstract: Simulation is increasingly used to support safety-related decision-making in road transport, particularly for the assessment and approval of automated driving systems (ADS). The complexity of ADS behavior and size of their operational design domains make exclusive reliance on physical testing impractical, leading to extensive use of virtual testing (VT) during the approval phase. This shift raises critical questions regarding the credibility of modelling and simulation (M&S) results used to support road safety decisions. Current VT accreditation approaches in the ADS domain typically rely on validation-only practices, which have been shown to scale poorly when applied to complex, multi-tool simulation environments. To address this limitation, this paper proposes a risk-based framework for assessing the credibility of simulation toolchains used in ADS safety evaluation, drawing inspiration from established practices in other safety-critical domains, notably NASA's STD-7009 for models and simulations. The framework extends traditional verification and validation (V&V) by explicitly linking credibility requirements to the intended use of simulation outputs and to the safety criticality of the decisions they support within the approval process. It provides a lifecycle-oriented assessment scheme integrating toolchain management, modelling assumptions and limitations, verification, validation, and sensitivity analysis. Credibility acceptance thresholds are defined proportionally, allowing differentiated requirements depending on whether simulation is used for exploratory safety analysis, partial decision support, or as a substitute for physical testing. While demonstrated for ADS, the proposed approach is directly applicable to road safety and simulation studies where VT plays a central role in safety assessment and regulatory decision-making.

</details>


### [357] [Robot Aware Computational Design of Object Specific Passive Grippers for Additive Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03761)
*Abdullah Yahya Abdullah Omaisan,Ibrahim Sheikh Mohamed*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个端到端计算流水线，可将选定物体网格、物体状态和六轴机器人转换为物体专属、非驱动的、可增材制造的夹爪。流水线耦合了网格级RGB-D/ICP位姿配准、确定性表面接触采样、不确定性感知的力旋量筛选、六种被动捕获机构选择、物体贴合曲面合成、全姿态机器人逆运动学、扫描体积感知制造域、方向性熔融沉积有限元筛选及约束三维SIMP拓扑优化。四个归档夹爪样例通过名义拟合、不确定力旋量、运行时间扫描及基线/后拓扑FEA门限，但缺乏标定材料属性与仪器测试，处于数字筛选而非可操作发布状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有夹爪设计流程将抓取选择、工具几何、运动规划和结构设计视为相互独立的问题，缺乏将设计产物与源网格、物体位姿、机器人法兰、接触集和插入假设绑定的可追溯性。本文旨在打破这一割裂，提供一个端到端一体化设计方法，使每个导出的设计都能追溯到其原始输入假设。

Method: 方法包含十个关键环节：(1) 精确网格级RGB-D/ICP位姿配准；(2) 确定性表面接触采样；(3) 不确定性感知力旋量筛选；(4) 六种被动捕获机构选择；(5) 物体贴合曲面合成；(6) 全姿态机器人逆运动学求解；(7) 扫描体积感知制造域定义；(8) 方向性熔融沉积(FDM)有限元筛选；(9) 约束三维SIMP拓扑优化；(10) 可追溯设计标识符绑定。同时推导了配准、接触、装配公差、有限元和密度优化的方程，并证明了节点载荷保持、SIMP插值下单调柔度敏感性以及拓扑后处理后的体素域包含性三个数值性质。

Result: 四个归档的物体专属夹爪尝试（兔子、相机法兰、3DBenchy、多面半身像）均通过名义拟合、不确定力旋量、运行时间扫描及基线/后拓扑FEA门限。在故意放大的±3 mm、±5度位姿压力测试中，160个模拟试验中保留29-134个。重构后的拓扑保留92.0-97.6%的有限元域，因为功能区域受到保护。机器人照片定性展示了打印装配的结果。

Conclusion: 该流水线能生成概念上完整、可追溯的物体专属被动夹爪设计，且在数字仿真层面通过全部设计门限。然而，由于采用名义材料属性且缺乏按试样标定的仪器测试，四个设计均停留在数字筛选状态而非可操作发布。后续需要通过标定实验和仪器化测试来完成操作验证。

Abstract: This paper presents an end-to-end computational pipeline that converts a selected object mesh, a measured object state, and a selected six-axis robot into an object-specific, unactuated, additively manufacturable gripper. The method couples exact-mesh RGB-D/ICP pose registration, deterministic surface-contact sampling, uncertainty-aware wrench screening, selection among six passive capture mechanisms, object-conformal surface synthesis, full-orientation robot inverse kinematics, a swept-volume-aware manufacturing domain, directional fused-deposition finite-element screening, and constrained three-dimensional SIMP topology optimization. Unlike workflows that treat grasp selection, tool geometry, motion, and structural design as separate problems, every exported design is bound to the source mesh, object pose, robot flange, contact set, and insertion hypothesis by a traceable design identifier. We derive the implemented registration, contact, fit-tolerance, finite-element, and density-optimization equations and prove three properties of the numerical construction: nodal load preservation, monotonic compliance sensitivity under SIMP interpolation, and voxel-domain containment after topology post-processing. Four archived object-specific attempts - a rabbit, camera flange, 3DBenchy, and faceted bust - meet the nominal fit, uncertain-wrench, runtime-sweep, and baseline/post-topology FEA gates. A deliberately enlarged +/-3 mm, +/-5 degree pose stress check differentiates the designs, retaining 29-134 of 160 simulated trials. Their reconstructed topologies retain 92.0-97.6% of the FE domain because functional regions are protected. Archived robot photographs show the corresponding printed assemblies qualitatively, while nominal material properties and absent coupon-calibrated, instrumented tests keep all four at digital-screening status rather than operational release.

</details>


### [358] [A comparative study on the accuracy & repeatability of mobile robotic platforms for the delivery of precision NDE measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03794)
*SeyedMohammadAmin Nabi Pour,S. Gareth Pierce,Randika Vithanage,Ehsan Mohseni,David Carswell,Matthew Shields*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于激光跟踪仪的外部参照评估流程（精度约6微米），在统一协议下测量了五种商用移动机器人平台的静态与分段轨迹定位精度（KMP-1500、KMR、MiR250、Boston Dynamics Spot、Clearpath Husky），发现没有任何平台能从基座单独满足航空航天NDE的0.2-1.0 mm公差，并据此量化了各平台所需的补充传感余量（约1-2个数量级），为平台选型提供可复现依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人平台为非固定机械臂的航空航天大结构无损检测提供灵活替代方案，但其基座定位精度以及该精度应如何指导部署，尚未在统一的外部参照协议下被系统评估。

Method: 采用激光跟踪仪建立评估工作流（地面真值约6微米），在统一协议下测量五种商业移动平台的静态与分段轨迹定位精度；使用耦合多角标定恢复激光到机器人的变换及反射镜偏移，以全位姿普通最小二乘估计为主，鲁棒估计仅作为粗差检验。

Result: 静态定位精度中位数从8.2 mm（KMP-1500）到63.5 mm（Spot）不等，仅轮式里程计的Husky无法标定；动态路径跟踪以横向误差表征，对时间对齐不敏感，范围从6.9 mm（KMP-1500）到112.1 mm（Spot）；精度和可标定性均与定位能力（从最新LiDAR SLAM到无地图视觉里程计）相关。

Conclusion: 没有配置能单独从基座满足0.2-1.0 mm的航空航天NDE公差；结果作为设计输入，量化各平台所需的补充传感规模（最优平台约需提高一个数量级，最差近两个数量级），为平台选型提供可复现依据而非可行性声明。

Abstract: Mobile robotic platforms offer a flexible alternative to fixed manipulators for non-destructive evaluation (NDE) of large aerospace structures, but their base-positioning accuracy and how that accuracy should inform deployment have not been assessed under a common, externally referenced protocol. This work presents a laser tracker-based evaluation workflow (ground truth approximately 6 micrometers) that measures the static and segmented trajectory positioning accuracy of five commercial mobile platforms (KUKA KMP-1500, KUKA KMR, MiR250, Boston Dynamics Spot, Clearpath Husky) under a common protocol. A coupled multi-corner calibration recovers the laser-to-robot transformation and reflector offsets; ordinary least squares over all poses is used, with robust estimation retained only as a blunder check.
  Static positioning accuracy ranged from a median of 8.2 mm (KMP-1500) to 63.5 mm (Spot), with the wheel-odometry-only Husky uncalibratable. Dynamic path following was characterised by cross-track error; the component was insensitive to temporal alignment, which ranged from 6.9 mm (KMP-1500) to 112.1 mm (Spot). Both accuracy and calibratability tracked localisation capability, from the newest LiDAR SLAM platform to map-free visual odometry. No configuration meets the 0.2 to 1.0 mm aerospace NDE tolerance from the base alone; the results are framed as a design input that sizes the supplementary sensing each platform requires: roughly one order of magnitude for the best platform and nearly two for the worst, providing a reproducible basis for platform selection rather than a feasibility claim.

</details>


### [359] [FWBC-VLA: Force-Aware Whole-Body Compensation for Contact-Rich Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03889)
*Yutian Zhang,Siyuan Ma,Liwen Yang,Yang Li,Ce Hao,Haozhen Chi,Dong We,Qiaojun Yu,Dibo Hou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FWBC-VLA框架，桥接任务级VLA动作生成与低级全身补偿控制，用于轮腿机器人的接触丰富型腿部操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的腿部操作需要在语义动作生成与物理交互控制之间建立桥梁。现有VLA模型无法解释动作引发的物理交互，WBC策略无法区分任务相关交互力与外部扰动力，而直接使用力/力矩传感器会带来额外硬件成本与集成负担。

Method: 提出FWBC-VLA框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) HSR-Force无传感器残差力矩估计器，用于推断接触强度及其时间变化；(2) 将接触估计编码为token注入VLA动作专家解码过程，使策略感知接触开始、持续加载与释放；(3) 将本体感觉状态、Jacobian导出的机体坐标系力估计和估计接触状态联合输入补偿生成器产生校正动作，并与操作动作结合后交给WBC策略执行。

Result: 构建了含5000+episodes的WL&Arm数据集并对预训练VLA骨干进行全参数微调。在白板擦拭和带闭门器的开门等真实世界实验中验证了FWBC-VLA在接触丰富腿部操作中的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过无传感器的力感知方案，FWBC-VLA成功将高层任务语义与底层全身物理控制解耦又结合，实现了在无需额外力传感器硬件的情况下感知物理交互并做出稳健的接触丰富腿部操作。

Abstract: Contact-rich loco-manipulation requires a bridge between semantic action generation and physical interaction control. Existing Vision-language-action (VLA) models generate task-level actions from visual and linguistic observations, but cannot interpret the physical interactions induced by those actions. While the whole-body control (WBC) policy can stabilize the robot, it cannot distinguish task-relevant interaction forces from forces induced by external disturbances during manipulation. Although force/torque sensors provide direct measurements of physical interactions, retrofitting them entails additional hardware costs and substantial integration effort, particularly for platforms not designed with sensor integration in mind. To address this problem, we propose FWBC-VLA, a force-aware framework that bridges task-level VLA action generation and low-level whole-body compensation control for wheeled-legged robots. First, we introduce HSR-Force, a sensorless residual-torque estimator for inferring contact strength and its temporal variation. These contact estimates are then encoded as tokens and injected into the VLA action expert during action decoding, enabling the policy to perceive contact onset, sustained loading, and release. For loco-manipulation tasks, all parameters of the pretrained VLA backbone are fine-tuned on our WL\&Arm Dataset, which comprises more than 5,000 episodes. Moreover, the robot's proprioceptive state, the Jacobian-derived body-frame force estimate, and the estimated contact state are jointly fed into a compensation generator to produce corrective actions. The manipulation-centric actions are subsequently combined with the corrective actions and passed to the WBC policy for execution. Real-world experiments on whiteboard wiping and door opening with a door closer demonstrate the effectiveness of our FWBC-VLA in contact-rich loco-manipulation.

</details>


### [360] [A hybrid pipeline for dynamic ontology-based semantic mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03891)
*Konstantinos Dimitropoulos,Ioannis Hatzilygeroudis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合语义地图构建管线，融合外部标定相机（单应性投影）进行几何建图与定位，结合目标检测、持久目标跟踪和本体驱动的语义更新，构建动态语义世界模型，并利用线性回归修正真实世界坐标估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义建图对机器人在复杂环境中交互、操作和导航至关重要。现有管线大多采用几何建图与定位（SLAM）、感知、语义融合和语义表示的串行流程，但缺乏对上下文理解能力的提升。近年来虽有研究引入知识图谱或语义场景图等先验知识，仍需一个更完整的混合方案来整合外部感知、动态跟踪与语义推理。

Method: 系统采用混合管线，具体包括：(1) 使用外部标定相机通过单应性投影实现几何建图与定位；(2) 集成目标检测、持久目标跟踪技术；(3) 采用基于本体的语义更新机制，动态维护目标实例、空间属性和语义关系；(4) 引入线性回归模型对真实世界坐标估计值进行校正。本体因其层级结构、语义表达能力和对动态世界建模的支持而被选为知识表示形式。

Result: 该系统能够基于实时感官数据持续更新目标实例、空间属性与语义关系，构建出支持动态变化的语义世界模型，并通过线性回归校正提升坐标估计的精度。

Conclusion: 本文展示了结合外部视觉感知、持久跟踪和本体驱动的混合语义建图框架，本体作为知识表示形式在动态世界建模中展现了良好的层级结构化支撑与语义表达能力。

Abstract: Semantic mapping plays a crucial role in the ability of a robot to interact with objects, operate and navigate a complex environment. The most common pipeline for semantic mapping consists of geometric mapping and localization (SLAM), perception, semantic fusion and semantic representation. However, more recent works also integrate a form of prior knowledge in their application, most notably knowledge graphs or semantic scene graphs, to improve contextual understanding of the environment. In this paper, we present a hybrid pipeline for semantic mapping. Our system incorporates an external calibrated camera using homography projection for geometric mapping and localization, combined with object detection, persistent object tracking and ontology driven semantic updates to build a dynamic semantic world model. Linear regression models are also used for correction of the estimated values of real world coordinates. The system continuously updates object instances, spatial properties and semantic relations based on real time sensory data. Ontologies are selected as form of knowledge representation due to their hierarchical structure, semantic expressiveness and support for dynamic world modelling.

</details>


### [361] [Revisiting Topological Graphs for Macro Action based Closed-loop Reinforcement Learning of Vision Language Navigation in Continuous Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03906)
*Shuhao Ye,Sitong Mao,Yuxiang Cui,Yufei Wei,Xuan Yu,Shichao Zhai,Wen Chen,Shunbo Zhou,Rong Xiong,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出将连续环境下的视觉语言导航（VLN-CE）重构为分层马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），将高层规划与低层控制解耦，在拓扑图上定义宏动作空间，并引入基于图的PPO优化与动作感知的价值头，在R2R-CE和RxR-CE基准上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模仿学习（IL）流程在VLN-CE闭环设置中存在局限：行为克隆面临分布偏移问题，DAgger在轨迹偏离后专家动作变得模糊。强化学习虽天然适合此问题，但直接应用于微动作空间因奖励稀疏而样本效率低下，亟需一种能够压缩决策时域并解决稀疏奖励瓶颈的替代方案。

Method: 将VLN-CE重构为分层MDP：高层策略在环境抽象出的拓扑图上以边界节点作为宏动作空间进行决策；训练免调的低层控制器充当状态转移函数，从而显著压缩决策时域使闭环RL变得可行。同时提出动作感知价值头（action-aware value head）来在动态边界动作空间下有效评估状态价值，并配合基于图的PPO算法进行RL优化。

Result: 大量实验验证了所提出架构的有效性，在R2R-CE和RxR-CE两个基准上均取得了当前最优（state-of-the-art）的性能表现。

Conclusion: 通过分层MDP解耦高层规划与低层控制，并借助拓扑图宏动作空间压缩决策时域，使得闭环RL在VLN-CE中变得可行且高效，验证了该范式相较现有IL方法的优越性。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation in Continuous Environments (VLN-CE) requires an agent to follow natural language instructions through unseen environments. Existing imitation learning (IL) pipelines struggle in this closed-loop setting: behavior cloning suffers from distribution shift, and DAgger's expert actions become ambiguous upon trajectory deviation. While Reinforcement Learning (RL) offers a natural paradigm to address this, directly applying RL to micro action spaces is sample-inefficient due to reward sparsity. To overcome this bottleneck, we reformulate VLN-CE as a Hierarchical Markov Decision Process (MDP), explicitly decoupling high-level planning from low-level control. By abstracting the environment into a topological graph, our high-level policy operates on a macro action space of frontier nodes, with a training-free low-level controller acting as its state transition, which significantly compresses the decision horizon and makes closed-loop RL tractable. To support RL optimization on the macro MDP, we propose an action-aware value head to effectively evaluate state values under the dynamic frontier action space, powering a graph-based PPO. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our architecture. Finally, our model achieves state-of-the-art performance on the R2R-CE and RxR-CE benchmarks.

</details>


### [362] [Toward Unified Robot Learning: Bridging Representation, Vision-Language-Action, and World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03927)
*Shaunak A. Mehta,Ananya Hazarika,Haochen Zhang,Fan Yang,Ryo Moriyama,Wenkai Li,Yash Patel,Kanata Suzuki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于机器人学习的综述论文，提出从感知（表征学习）、行动（VLA模型）和推理（世界模型）三条互补轴线统一审视机器人学习系统，分析各组件之间的交互、共同局限性以及未来走向统一化、物理基础化和概率化机器人学习的方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习领域的表征学习、VLA（视觉-语言-动作）模型和世界模型三大范式通常是孤立发展的，导致系统碎片化，难以应对泛化、长时序推理与规划、非结构化环境部署等挑战。缺乏一个统一视角来整合这些方法。

Method: 通过组织现有方法至三条互补轴线（理解—表征学习、行动—VLA模型、推理—世界模型），引入结构化分类体系，涵盖环境表征、策略学习与预测建模中的关键设计选择；在此基础上分析组件间交互、总结通用局限性并识别新兴一体化系统的趋势。

Result: 识别出机器人学习领域的主要挑战，包括不确定性量化、分布外泛化、跨本体迁移、长上下文理解和长视野规划，并论证这些挑战不仅源于单个组件的局限，更源于感知、行动与推理之间缺乏集成。

Conclusion: 提出未来应朝向统一化、物理基础化和概率化的机器人学习发展，以构建维持一致内部表征并支持真实环境中长时间交互决策的稳健机器人系统。

Abstract: For robots to operate reliably in real-world environments, they need to perceive their surroundings, act, and reason about the consequences of those actions. Rapid progress in the domains of representation learning, VLA models, and world models has significantly enhanced the capabilities of robot learning systems, enabling robots to work in increasingly complex environments. However, these paradigms are typically developed in isolation, resulting in fragmented systems that struggle with generalization, long-horizon temporal reasoning and planning, and deployment in unstructured environments. In this survey, we present a unified perspective on robot learning by organizing the existing methods along three complementary axes: understanding through representation learning, acting through VLA models, and reasoning through world models. We introduce a structured taxonomy that captures key design choices in environment representation, policy learning, and predictive modeling, and summarize the recent progress in these domains. Beyond classifying the existing works, we analyze how these components interact, discuss common limitations, and highlight emerging trends towards more integrated systems. Through this lens, we identify the challenges in the domain of robot learning, including uncertainty quantification, out-of-distribution generalization, cross-embodiment transfer, long-context understanding, and long-horizon planning. We argue that these challenges arise not only from limitations within individual components but also from the lack of integration across perception, action, and reasoning. Building on this analysis, we outline future directions towards unified, physically grounded, and probabilistic robot learning to develop robust robotic systems that maintain consistent internal representations and support decision making over extended interactions in real-world environments.

</details>


### [363] [Automated Weld Seam Recognition and 3D Mapping for Robotic Post Processing Using Photogrammetry and Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03970)
*Augustin Raju,Abilash Madavath,Chandra Yuvesh Aubeeluck,Nicolas Pyschny,Felix Hackelöer,Florian Zwanzig*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于视觉的实验性管道，用于焊缝的近似定位，作为高精度测量前的预处理阶段，以减少大工件表面扫描的工作量和数据量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于大型工件，使用高精度激光扫描仪或结构光传感器进行完整表面扫描耗时且产生大量不相关数据，因此需要一种初步的焊缝近似定位方法以减少整体扫描工作量并提高数据采集效率。

Method: 该方法包括：从多个视角采集工件图像，使用语义分割从图像中识别焊缝，利用摄影测量技术重建工件模型，并将识别出的焊缝投影到重建的三维模型中。

Result: 该管道能够在大规模高精度测量之前实现焊缝的近似定位，从而减少扫描区域并提高整体数据采集效率。

Conclusion: 本文提出的视觉管道可作为自动化机器人后处理操作（如打磨、精加工和检测）中高精度测量前的有效预定位阶段，降低扫描成本并提升效率。

Abstract: Accurate identification of weld seam geometries is essential for automated robotic post processing operations such as grinding, finishing, and inspection. For large workpieces, complete surface scanning using high precision laser scanners or structured light sensors can be time consuming and often generates substantial amount of data that are not relevant. This paper presents an experimental vision based pipeline for the approximate localization of weld seams. This serves as a preliminary stage before high precision measurement. The proposed approach aims to reduce the overall scanning effort and data acquisition efficiency. The proposed method includes capturing images of the workpiece from multiple viewpoints, identifying weld seams from the images using semantic segmentation, reconstructing the workpiece using photogrammetry, and projection of identified weld seams into the reconstructed model.

</details>


### [364] [MulDP: Multimodal Diffusion Policy for Autonomous Quadruped Parkour Navigation across Complex Terrains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03984)
*Kangmai Hu,Yueqi Zhang,Peng Zhai,Xiaoyi Wei,Jiabin Hu,Zhixiang Liu,Quancheng Qian,Lihua Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多模态扩散策略（MulDP），将视觉感知、机器人本体感知与目标信息融合，生成时间连贯且具有前瞻性的导航速度指令，实现四足机器人自主跑酷导航；同时构建了首个四足跑酷导航数据集（QPND），并在仿真与真实实验中验证了其在复杂地形上的鲁棒长程导航能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然四足机器人在跑酷运动上已展现出出色的敏捷性，但大多数系统仍依赖人工进行高层规划，自主跑酷导航尚未得到充分探索。其核心挑战包括细粒度的速度调节、长时域的前瞻性行为，以及感知与具身执行之间的紧密耦合。

Method: 提出多模态扩散策略（MulDP），将视觉感知与机器人本体感知及目标信息进行集成，通过扩散模型生成时间上连贯且具有前瞻性的导航速度指令，从而将感知与具身控制紧密结合。此外，构建了首个四足跑酷导航数据集（QPND），涵盖多种导航行为和复杂地形以支持模型训练。

Result: 在广泛的仿真与真实世界实验中，MulDP实现了鲁棒的长时间跨度自主导航，并能有效穿越复杂地形。

Conclusion: MulDP通过多模态扩散策略有效解决了四足机器人自主跑酷导航中速度调控、前瞻性和感知-执行耦合等关键难题，验证了该框架在复杂地形下长程自主导航的可行性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Quadruped robots have demonstrated impressive agility in parkour locomotion across complex terrains. However, most systems still rely on human intervention for high-level planning, and autonomous parkour navigation remains underexplored. The key challenges include fine-grained velocity regulation, long-horizon anticipatory behaviors, and tight coupling between perception and embodied execution. To address these challenges, we propose a Multimodal Diffusion Policy (MulDP) that integrates visual perception with robot proprioception and goal information to generate temporally coherent and anticipatory navigation velocity commands, tightly coupling perception with embodied control to enable robust autonomous navigation. To support the training of MulDP, we construct the first Quadruped Parkour Navigation Dataset (QPND), a multimodal dataset that encompasses diverse navigation behaviors and complex terrains. Extensive simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate that MulDP enables robust long-horizon autonomous navigation and effective traversal across complex terrains.

</details>


### [365] [Adaptive Vision-Language Grasping via Composable Foundation Priors and Generalizable Grasp Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04096)
*Sixu Yan,Shikang Wang,Binhua Huang,Xuanlai Tang,Guohua Fan,Fan Huang,Haoxuan Li,Yongkang Li,Yuhan Li,Bencheng Liao,Zeyu Zhang,Wenyu Liu,Hangxin Liu,Xinggang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaRoboVLG，一种任务自适应的视觉-语言-抓取（VLG）框架，通过将物理可行的抓取合成与任务相关理解解耦，实现了跨不同机器人手的通用抓取合成，并支持在不重新训练基础抓取策略的情况下集成多种先验知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLG方法将基础模型与端到端抓取策略紧密耦合，导致抓取策略缺乏跨手泛化能力、难以适应不同任务场景，且任务相关的语义理解难以独立演进和模块化扩展。

Method: AdaRoboVLG包含两个核心部分：（1）一个高效可泛化的基础策略，通过显式运动学映射与基于力闭合的稳定性估计生成并评估物理可行的抓取候选；（2）多个专责的基础模型模块，提供可组合的空间、认知与时间先验，以即插即用方式集成到抓取合成过程中，无需重训底层抓取策略即可实现上下文自适应抓取。

Result: 通过仿真与真实世界实验证明：（i）基础策略学习高效且具有强跨手泛化能力；（ii）框架能有效利用空间、认知和时间先验解决三类典型抓取挑战，且抓取合成性能不逊于最先进方法；（iii）多种先验可联合作用，实现在杂乱动态环境中的功能性抓取。

Conclusion: 将物理抓取合成与任务相关理解解耦是一种可扩展的机器人抓取范式，未来基础模型的进步可直接转化为抓取能力的提升，而无需重新设计或训练底层抓取策略。
